2025-06-14T22:54:02.1491980Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1492153Z <pr_title>
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1492686Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1493287Z </pr_title>
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1493462Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1493585Z <pr_description>
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1494907Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1495989Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1496125Z ## Problem
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1496610Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1497225Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1497878Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1498608Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1499143Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1499704Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1500400Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1500885Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1501691Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1502548Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1502679Z ## Solution
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1502869Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1503201Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1503863Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1504394Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1505687Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1507009Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1508030Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1508509Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1508687Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1509149Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1509443Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1509597Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1509966Z ```bash
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1510769Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1511878Z ```
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1512439Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1512739Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1513130Z ```bash
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1514149Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1515565Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1515952Z ```
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1516122Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1516363Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1516753Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1516910Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1517823Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1518634Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1519052Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1519504Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1520544Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1521510Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1522450Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1523503Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1524125Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1525050Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1525789Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1525925Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1526085Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1526090Z 
2025-06-14T22:54:02.1526217Z </pr_description>

2025-06-14T22:55:10.2468807Z     151.- **Python**: 3.10+

2025-06-14T22:55:10.2471581Z     156.## Background
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2472186Z     157.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2472871Z     158.This solution addresses the complexity of installing Prefect on OT-2 systems where:
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2473653Z     159.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2474199Z     160.- Standard pip install fails due to compilation requirements
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2475164Z     161.- pendulum (Prefect dependency) requires Rust compilation 
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2475872Z     162.- ARMv7l wheels are not readily available
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2476494Z     163.- Limited system tools (no git, bash, venv)
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2477067Z     164.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2477804Z     165.The pre-built wheels in this repository eliminate these issues by providing compatible binaries.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2478640Z     166.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2479044Z     167.## Support
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2479467Z     168.
2025-06-14T22:55:10.2479890Z     169.If you encounter issues:
